- **oauth-refresh-token-single-use-debug-burns-cred**: OAuth refresh tokens (GitHub, Notion, Slack, …) are SINGLE-USE — calling `provider.refresh(refresh_token)` from a debug REPL or one-shot script without persisting the returned new tokens to DB silently invalidates the credential.
